# Seed 72 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the outputs checked, jitfuse enabled, and contention ruled out (Iteration 520)

`seed 72 9h widths noevolved then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip
~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16 then experiment contention.py --sample 16` at 6553c7ab (Iteration 516). 26,244
designs, generations 0-102 (21,557 alive, 4,655 screened); islands off. The front 185 designs. Its speed is over the
seven workloads, as seed 71's - the two compare; runs before 71 compare on sizes only.

**The outputs checked** (516): 31 designs died "wrong output: apps", every one cpt16 - the 791 of seed 71 that timed a
failing run no longer can; 67 cpt16 designs alive (their applications right at 64 bits) - and those 67 fail at 32 bits,
"wrong output: apps" (rule 5's 67: the same reach, refused out loud, at 4-byte cells - a known cause now).

**jitfuse**, enabled for this run: on 29 of the front's 115 JIT designs, 3 of its fastest tenth (18) - the prediction
(half of the JIT designs, most of the fastest tenth) fails. jitmul on 46 designs (seed 71: 72); jitexec 3, still with its
585 KB table (fixed in the after-seed-72 batch); jitdoes 2, jitcfa 4, jitrp 9.

**Contention ruled out** (the experiment of 514): the 16 fastest measured as the run measures them, alone and beside
three co-runners - loaded / alone 1.012 in the median (0.978-1.054). Both are ~7% slower than in the run (alone 1.069,
loaded 1.080): the fast end's offset is between the run and after it, with or without co-runners - not contention.

Facts: the fastest c3a18bd60b 0.1485 (seed 71's 0.1426 at generation 130; this run reached generation 102: 2,916
designs an hour against 3,273); the smallest 14,079 bytes (seed 71: 14,210); appbench.py on 16 of the front - nine
applications now (wordfreq, minif) - the fastest's geometric mean 0.275 of s6, Spearman with the in-run speed 0.797,
every output right; held out, sieve's Spearman 0.961 (fastest tenth -0.001), regret +2.9%; the fast end 1.073 (rule 3);
gene votes - freeze candidates rtfuse (12 of 12), rtimm (12 of 12), jitret (11 of 12).

---

# Evolved VM designs

21589 designs evaluated, 21557 alive, 4655 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 2dc1cc9d42, speed 0.152, binary + image 34,166, memory 604 KB - Q 0.289 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
